--// Functions
function placementVector(L_24_arg1, L_25_arg2, L_26_arg3, L_27_arg4)
	if L_25_arg2 then
		local L_28_ = L_26_arg3 + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)

		local L_29_ = Ray.new(L_28_, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0))
		local L_30_, L_31_, L_32_ = workspace:FindPartOnRay(L_29_, L_24_arg1)

		local L_33_ = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0):Cross(L_32_)
		local L_34_ = math.asin(L_33_.magnitude)
 
		L_24_arg1:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(L_24_arg1.PrimaryPart.CFrame:lerp(CFrame.new(L_31_ + L_32_ * L_24_arg1.PrimaryPart.Size.y / 2) * CFrame.fromAxisAngle(L_33_.magnitude == 0 and Vector3.new(1) or L_33_.unit, L_34_) * L_20_ * L_19_, L_27_arg4))
	end
end

L_2_.ChildAdded:connect(function(L_35_arg1)
	if L_35_arg1:IsA('Tool') and L_35_arg1:FindFirstChild('ACS_Setup') and L_10_.Health > 0 and require(L_35_arg1.ACS_Setup).Type == 'Build' and L_14_.BuildingEnabled then
		L_17_ = L_35_arg1
		
		L_22_ = L_8_:WaitForChild('PlacementUI'):clone()
		L_22_.Parent = L_1_.PlayerGui
		L_22_.Frame.Visible = true
		
		for L_36_forvar1, L_37_forvar2 in pairs(L_9_:GetChildren()) do
			if L_37_forvar2:IsA('Model') and L_37_forvar2.PrimaryPart then
				local L_38_ = L_22_:WaitForChild('Template'):WaitForChild('TemplateButton'):clone()
				L_38_.Parent = L_22_:WaitForChild('Frame'):WaitForChild('AssetListFrame')
				L_38_.Visible = true
				L_38_.Name = L_37_forvar2.Name
				L_38_.Text = L_37_forvar2.Name
				
				L_38_.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
					if not L_16_ then
						if L_15_.ResourcesEnabled then
							if L_1_.Team and L_1_.Team:FindFirstChild('Resources') and L_1_.Team.Resources.Value > 0 then
								L_18_ = L_9_:WaitForChild(L_38_.Name):clone()
								L_18_.Parent = workspace:FindFirstChild('Buildables') or workspace
								L_16_ = true
							end
						else
							L_18_ = L_9_:WaitForChild(L_38_.Name):clone()
							L_18_.Parent = workspace:FindFirstChild('Buildables') or workspace
							L_16_ = true
						end;
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end;
end)

L_2_.ChildRemoved:connect(function(L_39_arg1)
	if L_39_arg1 == L_17_ and L_14_.BuildingEnabled then
		L_22_:Destroy()
	end;

L_11_.InputBegan:connect(function(L_40_arg1, L_41_arg2)
	if not L_41_arg2 and L_14_.BuildingEnabled then
		if L_40_arg1.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
			if L_21_ == 'Rotate' then
				L_19_ = L_19_ * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(L_15_.RotInc), 0)
			elseif L_21_ == 'Move' then
				L_20_ = L_20_ * CFrame.new(0, L_15_.MoveInc, 0)
			end
		end;
		
		if L_40_arg1.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
			if L_21_ == 'Rotate' then
				L_19_ = L_19_ * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(-L_15_.RotInc), 0)
			elseif L_21_ == 'Move' then
				L_20_ = L_20_ * CFrame.new(0, -L_15_.MoveInc, 0)
			end
		end;
		
		if L_40_arg1.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.G then
			if L_21_ == 'Rotate' then
				L_19_ = L_19_ * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(L_15_.RotInc))
			elseif L_21_ == 'Move' then
				L_20_ = L_20_ * CFrame.new(0, 0, L_15_.MoveInc)
			end
		end;
		
		if L_40_arg1.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.H then
			if L_21_ == 'Rotate' then
				L_19_ = L_19_ * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(-L_15_.RotInc))
			elseif L_21_ == 'Move' then
				L_20_ = L_20_ * CFrame.new(0, 0, -L_15_.MoveInc)
			end
		end;
		
		if L_40_arg1.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.V then
			if L_21_ == 'Rotate' then
				L_19_ = L_19_ * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(L_15_.RotInc), 0, 0)
			elseif L_21_ == 'Move' then
				L_20_ = L_20_ * CFrame.new(L_15_.MoveInc, 0, 0)
			end
		end;
		
		if L_40_arg1.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.B then
			if L_21_ == 'Rotate' then	
				L_19_ = L_19_ * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-L_15_.RotInc), 0, 0)
			elseif L_21_ == 'Move' then
				L_20_ = L_20_ * CFrame.new(-L_15_.MoveInc, 0, 0)
			end
		end;
	
		if L_40_arg1.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F then
			if L_21_ == 'Rotate' then
				L_21_ = 'Move'
			elseif L_21_ == 'Move' then
				L_21_ = 'Rotate'
			end
		end;
		
		if L_40_arg1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			if not L_18_ and not L_16_ then
					
			elseif L_18_ and L_16_ then
				local L_42_ = 'HalfRot'
				local L_43_ = false
				local L_44_ = L_18_:GetDescendants()
				for L_45_forvar1, L_46_forvar2 in pairs(L_44_) do
					if L_46_forvar2:IsA('Seat') and L_46_forvar2.Name == 'WBTurretSeat' then
						L_43_ = true
						local L_47_ = L_1_.PlayerGui:WaitForChild('PlacementUI'):WaitForChild('OptionFrame')
						L_47_.Position = UDim2.new(0, L_4_.X - L_47_.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, L_4_.Y - L_47_.AbsoluteSize.Y)
						L_47_.Visible = true
						
						for L_48_forvar1, L_49_forvar2 in pairs(L_47_:GetChildren()) do
							if L_49_forvar2:IsA('TextButton') then
								L_49_forvar2.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
									if L_49_forvar2.Name == 'FRot' then
										L_42_ = 'FullRot'
									elseif L_49_forvar2.Name == 'HRot' then
										L_42_ = 'HalfRot'
									end
									L_47_.Visible = false
									L_47_.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end;
				
				if (L_4_.Hit.p - L_2_:WaitForChild('HumanoidRootPart').Position).magnitude <= L_15_.MaxDist then
					L_23_:FireServer(L_18_.Name, L_18_.PrimaryPart.CFrame, L_4_.Target, L_43_, L_42_, 'Place')
					L_18_:Destroy()
					L_19_ = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0)
					L_20_ = CFrame.new()
					L_16_ = false
				end;
			end
		end;
	end
end)

--// Functions
function UpdateTag(plr)
	if plr ~= L_1_ and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("TeamTagUI") then
		local Tag = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("TeamTagUI")
		if plr.Team == L_1_.Team then
			Tag.Enabled = true
			if plr.Character:FindFirstChild("ACS_Client") and plr.Character.ACS_Client:FindFirstChild("FireTeam") and plr.Character.ACS_Client.FireTeam.SquadName.Value ~= "" then
				Tag.Frame.Icon.ImageColor3 = plr.Character.ACS_Client.FireTeam.SquadColor.Value
			else
				Tag.Frame.Icon.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
			end
		else
			Tag.Enabled = false
		end
	end
end

--// Player Events

game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:connect(function()
	for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
		UpdateTag(v)
	end
end)--!strict

local AmmoProfiles = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("AmmoProfiles"))

local BallisticsResolver = {}

local function shallowCopy(source)
	local copy = {}
	if source then
		for key, value in pairs(source) do
			copy[key] = value
		end
	end
	return copy
end

function BallisticsResolver.Resolve(weaponData, modTable)
	local ammoId = weaponData.AmmoProfile or weaponData.AmmoType or "556x45_FMJ"
	local ammo = AmmoProfiles.Get(ammoId) or AmmoProfiles.Get("556x45_FMJ")

	local resolvedModTable = shallowCopy(modTable)
	resolvedModTable.DamageMod = (resolvedModTable.DamageMod or 1) * (ammo.DamageMult or 1)
	resolvedModTable.minDamageMod = (resolvedModTable.minDamageMod or 1) * (ammo.MinDamageMult or 1)
	resolvedModTable.MuzzleVelocity = (resolvedModTable.MuzzleVelocity or 1) * (ammo.VelocityMult or 1)
	resolvedModTable.Penetration = (resolvedModTable.Penetration or 1) * (ammo.PenetrationMult or 1)

	local muzzleVelocity = math.max(
		1,
		(weaponData.MuzzleVelocity or 2000)
			* (modTable and (modTable.MuzzleVelocity or 1) or 1)
			* (ammo.VelocityMult or 1)
	)

	local bulletDrop = (weaponData.BulletDrop or 1) * (ammo.DropMult or 1)
	local currentZero = weaponData.CurrentZero or 0
	local zeroOffset = (((bulletDrop * currentZero / 4) / muzzleVelocity) / 2) * (ammo.ZeroingScale or 1)

	return {
		AmmoId = ammoId,
		Ammo = ammo,
		ServerModTable = resolvedModTable,
		BasePenetration = resolvedModTable.Penetration or 1,

		MuzzleVelocity = muzzleVelocity,
		BulletDrop = bulletDrop,
		GravityScale = ammo.GravityScale or 1,
		Drag = ammo.Drag or 0,
		SpreadMult = ammo.SpreadMult or 1,
		SuppressionMult = ammo.SuppressionMult or 1,
		PenetrationIndex = ((weaponData.WallbangPenetration or ((weaponData.BulletPenetration or 0) * 0.18)) * (ammo.PenetrationMult or 1)),
		MaxDistance = ammo.MaxDistance or 7000,
		WhizzRadius = ammo.WhizzRadius or 25,
		ImpactProfile = ammo.ImpactProfile or "Rifle",
		ZeroOffset = zeroOffset,
	}
end

return BallisticsResolver--!strict

local DonorAimFatigue = {}
DonorAimFatigue.__index = DonorAimFatigue

local clamp = math.clamp
local max = math.max
local min = math.min

local DEFAULTS = {
	MaxAimStamina = 100,
	StaminaDrainRate = 1, -- Было 5 (уменьшено в 5 раз)
	StaminaRegenRate = 2.4, -- Было 12 (соразмерно уменьшено)

	IdleAimThreshold = 6,
	IdleAimRamp = 6,

	StaminaFactor = 1.8,
	MoveDrainMultiplier = 1.2,
	SteadyDrainMultiplier = 0.75,
	SteadyReduction = 0.036, -- Было 0.18 (уменьшено в 5 раз)
}

function DonorAimFatigue.new(settings)
	local self = setmetatable({}, DonorAimFatigue)

	self.settings = settings or {}

	self.maxAimStamina = self.settings.MaxAimStamina or DEFAULTS.MaxAimStamina
	self.staminaDrainRate = self.settings.StaminaDrainRate or DEFAULTS.StaminaDrainRate
	self.staminaRegenRate = self.settings.StaminaRegenRate or DEFAULTS.StaminaRegenRate
	self.idleAimThreshold = self.settings.IdleAimThreshold or DEFAULTS.IdleAimThreshold
	self.idleAimRamp = self.settings.IdleAimRamp or DEFAULTS.IdleAimRamp
	self.staminaFactor = self.settings.StaminaFactor or DEFAULTS.StaminaFactor

	self.currentStamina = self.maxAimStamina
	self.idleAimTime = 0
	self.lastFatigueScale = 1

	return self
end

function DonorAimFatigue:Update(dt, isAiming, isSteady, isMoving)
	dt = math.clamp(dt or 0, 0.0001, 0.1)

	if isAiming then
		local drain = self.staminaDrainRate
		if isMoving then
			drain *= DEFAULTS.MoveDrainMultiplier
		end
		if isSteady then
			drain *= DEFAULTS.SteadyDrainMultiplier
		end

		self.currentStamina = max(0, self.currentStamina - drain * dt)

		if not isMoving then
			self.idleAimTime += dt
		else
			self.idleAimTime = 0
		end
	else
		self.currentStamina = min(self.maxAimStamina, self.currentStamina + self.staminaRegenRate * dt)
		self.idleAimTime = 0
	end

	self.lastFatigueScale = self:GetFatigueScale(isSteady)
	return self.lastFatigueScale
end

function DonorAimFatigue:GetStaminaAlpha()
	if self.maxAimStamina <= 0 then
		return 1
	end

	return clamp(self.currentStamina / self.maxAimStamina, 0, 1)
end

function DonorAimFatigue:GetFatigueScale(isSteady)
	local staminaAlpha = self:GetStaminaAlpha()

	-- Коэффициент 0.65 уменьшен до 0.13 (в 5 раз)
	local staminaPenalty = (1 - staminaAlpha) * 0.13 * self.staminaFactor

	local longAimAlpha = 0
	if self.idleAimTime > self.idleAimThreshold then
		longAimAlpha = clamp((self.idleAimTime - self.idleAimThreshold) / self.idleAimRamp, 0, 1)
	end

	-- Коэффициент 0.55 уменьшен до 0.11 (в 5 раз)
	local longAimPenalty = longAimAlpha * 0.11
	local steadyReduction = isSteady and DEFAULTS.SteadyReduction or 0

	return max(0.65, 1 + staminaPenalty + longAimPenalty - steadyReduction)
end

function DonorAimFatigue:Reset()
	self.currentStamina = self.maxAimStamina
	self.idleAimTime = 0
	self.lastFatigueScale = 1
end

return DonorAimFatigue--!strict

local DonorSpring = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("DonorSpring"))

local DonorBreath = {}
DonorBreath.__index = DonorBreath

local sin = math.sin
local cos = math.cos
local pi = math.pi
local max = math.max

local DEFAULTS = {
	BreathCycleHip = 1.45,
	BreathCycleAim = 1.05,

	-- Значения ниже уменьшены в 4 раза
	HipScale = 0.0875, -- Было 0.35
	AimScale = 0.25,   -- Было 1.0
	MovePenalty = 0.7,

	PosXScale = 0.0875, -- Было 0.35
	PosYScale = 0.25,   -- Было 1.0
	PosZScale = 0.0375, -- Было 0.15

	RotPitchScale = 0.25, -- Было 1.0
	RotYawScale = 0.1375, -- Было 0.55
	RotRollScale = 0.2,   -- Было 0.8
}

function DonorBreath.new(settings)
	local self = setmetatable({}, DonorBreath)

	self.settings = settings or {}
	self.time = 0

	self.posSpring = DonorSpring.new(
		self.settings.BreathPosSpringFrequency or 4.0,
		self.settings.BreathPosSpringDamping or 0.8,
		self.settings.BreathPosSpringMass or 1.0,
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	)

	self.rotSpring = DonorSpring.new(
		self.settings.BreathRotSpringFrequency or 4.0,
		self.settings.BreathRotSpringDamping or 0.8,
		self.settings.BreathRotSpringMass or 1.0,
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	)

	return self
end

function DonorBreath:Update(dt, isAiming, isMoving, intensityScale)
	dt = math.clamp(dt or 0, 0.0001, 0.05)

	self.time += dt * (isAiming and DEFAULTS.BreathCycleAim or DEFAULTS.BreathCycleHip)

	local posIntensity = ((self.settings.BreathIntensity and self.settings.BreathIntensity.Position) or 0.008)
	local rotIntensity = ((self.settings.BreathIntensity and self.settings.BreathIntensity.Rotation) or 0.003)

	local scale = (isAiming and DEFAULTS.AimScale or DEFAULTS.HipScale) * (intensityScale or 1)
	if isMoving then
		scale *= DEFAULTS.MovePenalty
	end

	local t = self.time * pi * 2

	local posTarget = Vector3.new(
		sin(t) * posIntensity * DEFAULTS.PosXScale,
		cos(t * 2) * posIntensity * DEFAULTS.PosYScale,
		sin(t * 0.5) * posIntensity * DEFAULTS.PosZScale
	) * scale

	local rotTarget = Vector3.new(
		cos(t * 2) * rotIntensity * DEFAULTS.RotPitchScale,
		sin(t) * rotIntensity * DEFAULTS.RotYawScale,
		sin(t * 0.5) * rotIntensity * DEFAULTS.RotRollScale
	) * scale

	self.posSpring:SetTarget(posTarget)
	self.rotSpring:SetTarget(rotTarget)

	local pos = self.posSpring:Update(dt)
	local rot = self.rotSpring:Update(dt)

	return CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(rot.X, rot.Y, rot.Z)
end

function DonorBreath:Reset()
	self.time = 0
	self.posSpring:Reset(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
	self.rotSpring:Reset(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
end

return DonorBreath--!strict

local DonorSpring = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("DonorSpring"))

local DonorFireKick = {}
DonorFireKick.__index = DonorFireKick

local DEFAULT_CONFIG = {
	PosSpringFrequency = 14.0,
	PosSpringDamping = 0.85,
	PosSpringMass = 1.0,

	RotSpringFrequency = 11.0,
	RotSpringDamping = 0.82,
	RotSpringMass = 1.0,

	AimReductionFactor = 0.65,
	KickMultiplier = 1.0,

	PositionScale = Vector3.new(1.2, 1.35, 1.5),
	RotationScale = Vector3.new(0.65, 0.35, 0.18),

	SnapThreshold = 0.00008,
}

local function mergeConfig(config)
	local finalConfig = {}

	for key, value in pairs(DEFAULT_CONFIG) do
		finalConfig[key] = value
	end

	if config then
		for key, value in pairs(config) do
			finalConfig[key] = value
		end
	end

	return finalConfig
end

local function zeroIfTinyVector3(value, velocity, threshold)
	if value.Magnitude < threshold and velocity.Magnitude < threshold then
		return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end

	return value
end

function DonorFireKick.new(config)
	local self = setmetatable({}, DonorFireKick)

	self.config = mergeConfig(config)

	self.posSpring = DonorSpring.new(
		self.config.PosSpringFrequency,
		self.config.PosSpringDamping,
		self.config.PosSpringMass,
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	)

	self.rotSpring = DonorSpring.new(
		self.config.RotSpringFrequency,
		self.config.RotSpringDamping,
		self.config.RotSpringMass,
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	)

	self.camKickTarget = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

	return self
end

function DonorFireKick:FireKick(x, y, z)
	x = x or 0
	y = y or 0
	z = z or 0

	local impulse = Vector3.new(x, y, z) * self.config.KickMultiplier
	local posScale = self.config.PositionScale
	local rotScale = self.config.RotationScale

	self.posSpring:Shove(Vector3.new(
		impulse.X * posScale.X,
		impulse.Y * posScale.Y,
		impulse.Z * posScale.Z
		))

	self.rotSpring:Shove(Vector3.new(
		-impulse.Y * rotScale.X,
		impulse.X * rotScale.Y,
		impulse.X * rotScale.Z
		))

	self.camKickTarget = impulse
end

function DonorFireKick:Update(dt, aimFactor)
	dt = math.clamp(dt or 0, 0.0001, 0.05)
	aimFactor = math.clamp(aimFactor or 0, 0, 1)

	local aimScale = 1 - (aimFactor * (1 - self.config.AimReductionFactor))

	local pos = self.posSpring:Update(dt) * aimScale
	local rot = self.rotSpring:Update(dt) * aimScale

	pos = zeroIfTinyVector3(pos, self.posSpring:GetVelocity(), self.config.SnapThreshold)
	rot = zeroIfTinyVector3(rot, self.rotSpring:GetVelocity(), self.config.SnapThreshold)

	local camLerpAlpha = math.clamp(dt * 20, 0, 1)
	self.camKickTarget = self.camKickTarget:Lerp(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), camLerpAlpha)

	if self.camKickTarget.Magnitude < self.config.SnapThreshold then
		self.camKickTarget = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end

	return CFrame.new(pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z) * CFrame.Angles(rot.X, rot.Y, rot.Z)
end

function DonorFireKick:Reset()
	self.posSpring:Reset(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
	self.rotSpring:Reset(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
	self.camKickTarget = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end

function DonorFireKick:GetKickIntensity()
	local pos = self.posSpring:GetPosition()
	return pos.Magnitude
end

function DonorFireKick:GetCameraKick()
	return self.camKickTarget.X, self.camKickTarget.Y
end

function DonorFireKick:IsFinished()
	return self.posSpring:IsAtRest()
		and self.rotSpring:IsAtRest()
		and self.camKickTarget.Magnitude < self.config.SnapThreshold
end

return DonorFireKicklocal DonorLikeRecoilModule = {}
DonorLikeRecoilModule.__index = DonorLikeRecoilModule

-- Donor-like recoil module matching ACS profile structure
-- Uses: kickStrength, kickRandomness, horizontalStrength, horizontalRandomness,
-- rollStrength, firstShotMultiplier, sprayIncrease, sprayDecayTime,
-- cameraShake, gunKickback, gunRise, usePattern, patternScale, patternSpeed,
-- patternRandomness, pattern, aimMultiplier, crouchMultiplier, moveMultiplier,
-- returnSpeed, returnSmoothness

local random = math.random
local abs = math.abs
local min = math.min
local max = math.max
local clamp = math.clamp
local clock = os.clock
local sin = math.sin
local cos = math.cos

local function createSpring(stiffness, damping)
	return {
		position = 0,
		velocity = 0,
		stiffness = stiffness or 75,
		damping = damping or 18
	}
end

local function updateSpring(spring, dt)
	local accel = -spring.stiffness * spring.position - spring.damping * spring.velocity
	spring.velocity = spring.velocity + accel * dt
	spring.position = spring.position + spring.velocity * dt
	
	if abs(spring.position) < 0.0001 and abs(spring.velocity) < 0.001 then
		spring.position = 0
		spring.velocity = 0
	end
	
	return spring.position
end

function DonorLikeRecoilModule.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, DonorLikeRecoilModule)
	
	-- Rotational springs (pitch, yaw, roll)
	self.pitchSpring = createSpring(78, 16)
	self.yawSpring = createSpring(65, 14)
	self.rollSpring = createSpring(55, 12)
	
	-- Positional springs (gun kickback/rise)
	self.posYSpring = createSpring(85, 15)  -- Vertical
	self.posZSpring = createSpring(95, 18)  -- Forward/back
	self.posXSpring = createSpring(70, 13)  -- Horizontal
	
	-- State tracking
	self.currentProfile = nil
	self.shotsFired = 0
	self.sprayAmount = 0
	self.lastShotTime = 0
	self.patternIndex = 0
	
	-- Camera recoil output
	self.cameraRecoilX = 0
	self.cameraRecoilY = 0
	
	return self
end

function DonorLikeRecoilModule:SetProfile(profile)
	self.currentProfile = profile
end

function DonorLikeRecoilModule:Kick(profile, gunModel, multiplier)
	if not profile then
		profile = self.currentProfile
	end
	if not profile then return end
	
	multiplier = multiplier or 1
	
	local currentTime = clock()
	local timeSinceLastShot = currentTime - self.lastShotTime
	
	-- Spray decay logic (donor: sprayDecayTime)
	local sprayDecayTime = profile.sprayDecayTime or 0.4
	if timeSinceLastShot > sprayDecayTime then
		-- Decay spray when not shooting
		self.sprayAmount = max(0, self.sprayAmount - timeSinceLastShot * 6.0)
		if self.sprayAmount < 0.05 then
			self.sprayAmount = 0
			self.shotsFired = 0
			self.patternIndex = 0
		end
	end
	
	self.lastShotTime = currentTime
	
	-- First shot multiplier (donor: firstShotMultiplier)
	local firstShotMult = 1
	if self.shotsFired == 0 and profile.firstShotMultiplier then
		firstShotMult = profile.firstShotMultiplier
	end
	
	self.shotsFired = self.shotsFired + 1
	
	-- Spray increase (donor: sprayIncrease)
	local sprayIncrease = profile.sprayIncrease or 0.052
	self.sprayAmount = self.sprayAmount + sprayIncrease / (1 + self.shotsFired * 0.03)
	
	-- Calculate kick values
	local kickStrength = profile.kickStrength or 0.038
	local kickRandomness = profile.kickRandomness or 0.11
	local verticalKick = kickStrength * (1 + (random() - 0.5) * kickRandomness) * firstShotMult * (1 + self.sprayAmount * 0.8)
	
	local horizontalStrength = profile.horizontalStrength or 0.028
	local horizontalRandomness = profile.horizontalRandomness or 0.1
	local horizontalKick = horizontalStrength * (random() * 2 - 1) * (1 + (random() - 0.5) * horizontalRandomness)
	
	-- Pattern-based recoil (donor: usePattern, pattern, patternScale, patternRandomness)
	if profile.usePattern and profile.pattern and #profile.pattern > 0 then
		self.patternIndex = min(self.patternIndex + 1, #profile.pattern)
		local patternPoint = profile.pattern[self.patternIndex]
		local patternScale = profile.patternScale or 0.03
		local patternRandomness = profile.patternRandomness or 0.08
		local patternNoise = 1 + (random() - 0.5) * patternRandomness * 0.8
		
		verticalKick = patternPoint.y * patternScale * patternNoise
		horizontalKick = patternPoint.x * patternScale * patternNoise
	end
	
	-- Pattern speed affects kick intensity (donor: patternSpeed)
	local patternSpeed = profile.patternSpeed or 20
	local speedModifier = patternSpeed * (min(timeSinceLastShot, 0.1) * 2 + 0.8)
	
	verticalKick = verticalKick * multiplier * speedModifier
	horizontalKick = horizontalKick * multiplier * speedModifier
	
	-- Apply to springs
	self.pitchSpring.velocity = self.pitchSpring.velocity + verticalKick
	self.yawSpring.velocity = self.yawSpring.velocity + horizontalKick
	
	-- Roll (donor: rollStrength)
	local rollStrength = profile.rollStrength or 0.014
	local rollKick = rollStrength * (random() * 2 - 1) * multiplier * speedModifier * 0.7 - horizontalKick * 0.25
	self.rollSpring.velocity = self.rollSpring.velocity + rollKick
	
	-- Gun kickback/rise (donor: gunKickback, gunRise)
	local gunKickback = abs(profile.gunKickback or 0.038)
	local gunRise = profile.gunRise or 0.02
	
	self.posZSpring.velocity = self.posZSpring.velocity - gunKickback * speedModifier * multiplier * 1.1
	self.posYSpring.velocity = self.posYSpring.velocity + gunRise * speedModifier * multiplier * 0.75
	self.posXSpring.velocity = self.posXSpring.velocity + horizontalKick * 0.25 * multiplier
	
	-- Camera recoil for external use
	self.cameraRecoilX = verticalKick * 0.9
	self.cameraRecoilY = horizontalKick * 0.85
end

function DonorLikeRecoilModule:Update(dt)
	dt = min(dt, 0.033)
	
	local profile = self.currentProfile or {}
	
	-- Apply return speed/smoothness if profile has them (donor: returnSpeed, returnSmoothness)
	local returnSpeed = profile.returnSpeed or 0.17
	local returnSmoothness = profile.returnSmoothness or 0.8
	
	-- Update springs with profile-based stiffness
	local springStiffness = profile.springStiffness or 75
	local springDamping = profile.springDamping or 16
	
	self.pitchSpring.stiffness = springStiffness
	self.pitchSpring.damping = springDamping
	self.yawSpring.stiffness = springStiffness * 0.85
	self.yawSpring.damping = springDamping * 0.9
	self.rollSpring.stiffness = springStiffness * 0.7
	self.rollSpring.damping = springDamping * 0.8
	
	local pitch = updateSpring(self.pitchSpring, dt)
	local yaw = updateSpring(self.yawSpring, dt)
	local roll = updateSpring(self.rollSpring, dt)
	
	-- Clamp pitch to prevent excessive upward climb
	local minPitch = profile.minPitch or -0.25
	local maxPitch = profile.maxPitch or 0.65
	local maxYaw = profile.maxYaw or 0.45
	local maxRoll = profile.maxRoll or 0.3
	
	pitch = clamp(pitch, minPitch, maxPitch)
	yaw = clamp(yaw, -maxYaw, maxYaw)
	roll = clamp(roll, -maxRoll, maxRoll)
	
	-- Positional springs
	local posX = updateSpring(self.posXSpring, dt)
	local posY = updateSpring(self.posYSpring, dt)
	local posZ = updateSpring(self.posZSpring, dt)
	
	-- Decay spray over time
	self.sprayAmount = max(0, self.sprayAmount - dt * 4.5)
	
	-- Camera shake (donor: cameraShake)
	local cameraShake = profile.cameraShake or 0.028
	local shakeIntensity = (abs(self.pitchSpring.velocity) + abs(self.yawSpring.velocity)) * cameraShake * 0.015
	local time = clock()
	
	-- Build final CFrame
	local posCF = CFrame.new(
		posX * 0.25 + sin(time * 47) * shakeIntensity,
		posY * 0.35 + cos(time * 38) * shakeIntensity,
		posZ * 0.4
	)
	local rotCF = CFrame.Angles(pitch, yaw, roll)
	
	return posCF * rotCF
end

function DonorLikeRecoilModule:GetCameraRecoil()
	return self.cameraRecoilX, self.cameraRecoilY
end

function DonorLikeRecoilModule:Reset()
	self.pitchSpring.position = 0
	self.pitchSpring.velocity = 0
	self.yawSpring.position = 0
	self.yawSpring.velocity = 0
	self.rollSpring.position = 0
	self.rollSpring.velocity = 0
	self.posXSpring.position = 0
	self.posXSpring.velocity = 0
	self.posYSpring.position = 0
	self.posYSpring.velocity = 0
	self.posZSpring.position = 0
	self.posZSpring.velocity = 0
	self.shotsFired = 0
	self.sprayAmount = 0
	self.patternIndex = 0
	self.cameraRecoilX = 0
	self.cameraRecoilY = 0
end

return DonorLikeRecoilModule--!strict
local abs = math.abs
local sin = math.sin
local cos = math.cos
local rad = math.rad
local clamp = math.clamp
local exp = math.exp

local DonorSpring = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("DonorSpring"))

local DonorMotionSway = {}
DonorMotionSway.__index = DonorMotionSway

local DEFAULTS = {
	UseLookLayer = true,

	MouseXScale = 1.0,
	MouseYScale = 0.77,

	LookPosSpringFrequency = 7.5,
	LookPosSpringDamping = 0.78,
	LookRotSpringFrequency = 7.0,
	LookRotSpringDamping = 0.74,

	WalkPosSpringFrequency = 5.6,
	WalkPosSpringDamping = 0.74,
	WalkRotSpringFrequency = 5.2,
	WalkRotSpringDamping = 0.70,

	HipDeadzoneDiv = 2.0,
	AimDeadzoneDiv = 5.5,

	HipDeadzoneClamp = 7.0,
	AimDeadzoneClamp = 1.5,

	HipReturnSpeed = 2.4,
	AimReturnSpeed = 10.0,

	HipLookScale = 1.0,
	AimLookScale = 0.32,

	CrouchScale = 0.82,
	SprintScale = 1.18,
	FatigueLookInfluence = 1.0,

	LookPosScaleX = 0.00040,
	LookPosScaleY = 0.00052,

	LookRotPitch = 0.95,
	LookRotYaw = 0.95,
	LookRotRoll = 0.00,

	MicroShovePos = 0.000020,
	MicroShoveRot = 0.00032,

	WalkBaseSpeed = 7.4,
	WalkSpeedGain = 5.8,
	WalkFullSpeed = 14.0,

	WalkPosX = 0.016,
	WalkPosY = 0.030,
	WalkPosZ = 0.010,

	WalkRotPitch = 0.85,
	WalkRotYaw = 0.55,
	WalkRotRoll = 1.25,

	AimWalkMultiplier = 0.55,
	IdleSwayAmount = 0.18,
}

local function alphaFromSpeed(speed: number, dt: number): number
	return 1 - exp(-speed * dt)
end

local function getSetting(settings: {[string]: any}, name: string)
	local value = settings[name]
	if value == nil then
		return DEFAULTS[name]
	end
	return value
end

function DonorMotionSway.new(settings)
	local self = setmetatable({}, DonorMotionSway)

	self.settings = settings or {}
	self.walkTime = 0
	self.lookOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)

	self.lookPosSpring = DonorSpring.new(
		getSetting(self.settings, "LookPosSpringFrequency"),
		getSetting(self.settings, "LookPosSpringDamping"),
		1.0,
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	)

	self.lookRotSpring = DonorSpring.new(
		getSetting(self.settings, "LookRotSpringFrequency"),
		getSetting(self.settings, "LookRotSpringDamping"),
		1.0,
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	)

	self.walkPosSpring = DonorSpring.new(
		getSetting(self.settings, "WalkPosSpringFrequency"),
		getSetting(self.settings, "WalkPosSpringDamping"),
		1.0,
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	)

	self.walkRotSpring = DonorSpring.new(
		getSetting(self.settings, "WalkRotSpringFrequency"),
		getSetting(self.settings, "WalkRotSpringDamping"),
		1.0,
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	)

	return self
end

function DonorMotionSway:Update(dt, mouseDelta, moveSpeed, isAiming, isSprinting, isCrouching, fatigueScale)
	dt = clamp(dt or 0.016, 0.0001, 0.05)
	mouseDelta = mouseDelta or Vector2.new(0, 0)

	local settings = self.settings
	local fatigue = fatigueScale or 1

	local md = Vector2.new(
		mouseDelta.X * getSetting(settings, "MouseXScale"),
		mouseDelta.Y * getSetting(settings, "MouseYScale")
	)

	local useLookLayer = getSetting(settings, "UseLookLayer")
	local lookScale = (isAiming and getSetting(settings, "AimLookScale") or getSetting(settings, "HipLookScale"))
	lookScale *= (1 + ((fatigue - 1) * getSetting(settings, "FatigueLookInfluence")))

	if isCrouching then
		lookScale *= getSetting(settings, "CrouchScale")
	end
	if isSprinting then
		lookScale *= getSetting(settings, "SprintScale")
	end

	if useLookLayer then
		local deadzoneDiv = isAiming and getSetting(settings, "AimDeadzoneDiv") or getSetting(settings, "HipDeadzoneDiv")
		local clampRadius = isAiming and getSetting(settings, "AimDeadzoneClamp") or getSetting(settings, "HipDeadzoneClamp")
		local returnSpeed = isAiming and getSetting(settings, "AimReturnSpeed") or getSetting(settings, "HipReturnSpeed")

		if not isAiming then
			self.lookOffset -= md / deadzoneDiv
		end

		if self.lookOffset.Magnitude > clampRadius then
			self.lookOffset = self.lookOffset.Unit * clampRadius
		end

		self.lookOffset = self.lookOffset:Lerp(
			Vector2.new(0, 0),
			alphaFromSpeed(returnSpeed, dt)
		)

		local lookPosTarget = Vector3.new(
			self.lookOffset.X * getSetting(settings, "LookPosScaleX"),
			-self.lookOffset.Y * getSetting(settings, "LookPosScaleY"),
			0
		) * lookScale

		local lookRotTarget = Vector3.new(
			rad(self.lookOffset.Y * getSetting(settings, "LookRotPitch")),
			rad(self.lookOffset.X * getSetting(settings, "LookRotYaw")),
			rad(self.lookOffset.X * getSetting(settings, "LookRotRoll"))
		) * lookScale

		self.lookPosSpring:SetTarget(lookPosTarget)
		self.lookRotSpring:SetTarget(lookRotTarget)

		if isAiming then
			local microPosImpulse = Vector3.new(
				-md.X * getSetting(settings, "MicroShovePos"),
				md.Y * getSetting(settings, "MicroShovePos"),
				0
			) * lookScale

			local microRotImpulse = Vector3.new(
				rad(-md.Y * getSetting(settings, "MicroShoveRot")),
				rad(-md.X * getSetting(settings, "MicroShoveRot") * 0.9),
				0
			) * lookScale

			self.lookPosSpring:Shove(microPosImpulse)
			self.lookRotSpring:Shove(microRotImpulse)
		end
	else
		self.lookOffset = self.lookOffset:Lerp(Vector2.new(0, 0), alphaFromSpeed(12, dt))
		self.lookPosSpring:SetTarget(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
		self.lookRotSpring:SetTarget(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
	end

	local speedAlpha = clamp((moveSpeed or 0) / getSetting(settings, "WalkFullSpeed"), 0, 1)
	local moveScale = speedAlpha

	if isAiming then
		moveScale *= getSetting(settings, "AimWalkMultiplier")
	end
	if isSprinting then
		moveScale *= getSetting(settings, "SprintScale")
	end
	if isCrouching then
		moveScale *= getSetting(settings, "CrouchScale")
	end

	if speedAlpha > 0.02 then
		self.walkTime += dt * (getSetting(settings, "WalkBaseSpeed") + speedAlpha * getSetting(settings, "WalkSpeedGain"))
	else
		self.walkTime += dt * getSetting(settings, "IdleSwayAmount")
	end

	local walkPosTarget = Vector3.new(
		sin(self.walkTime) * getSetting(settings, "WalkPosX"),
		abs(cos(self.walkTime * 2)) * getSetting(settings, "WalkPosY"),
		cos(self.walkTime) * getSetting(settings, "WalkPosZ")
	) * moveScale

	local walkRotTarget = Vector3.new(
		rad(cos(self.walkTime * 2) * getSetting(settings, "WalkRotPitch")),
		rad(sin(self.walkTime) * getSetting(settings, "WalkRotYaw")),
		rad(sin(self.walkTime) * getSetting(settings, "WalkRotRoll"))
	) * moveScale

	if speedAlpha < 0.02 then
		walkPosTarget = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		walkRotTarget = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end

	self.walkPosSpring:SetTarget(walkPosTarget)
	self.walkRotSpring:SetTarget(walkRotTarget)

	local lookPos = self.lookPosSpring:Update(dt)
	local lookRot = self.lookRotSpring:Update(dt)

	local walkPos = self.walkPosSpring:Update(dt)
	local walkRot = self.walkRotSpring:Update(dt)

	local lookCF = CFrame.new(lookPos) * CFrame.Angles(lookRot.X, lookRot.Y, lookRot.Z)
	local walkCF = CFrame.new(walkPos) * CFrame.Angles(walkRot.X, walkRot.Y, walkRot.Z)

	return lookCF, walkCF
end

function DonorMotionSway:Reset()
	self.walkTime = 0
	self.lookOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)

	self.lookPosSpring:Reset(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
	self.lookRotSpring:Reset(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
	self.walkPosSpring:Reset(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
	self.walkRotSpring:Reset(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
end

return DonorMotionSway--!strict

local DonorRecoilSettings = {}

DonorRecoilSettings.RecoilStateMultipliers = {
	Aim = 0.35,
	Crouch = 0.75,
	Prone = 0.55,
	Move = 1.15,
	Sprint = 1.45,
	Jump = 1.35,
	FirstShot = 1.25,
	Suppressed = 0.88,
}

DonorRecoilSettings.SwaySpringFrequency = 1.8
DonorRecoilSettings.SwaySpringDamping = 0.85
DonorRecoilSettings.SwaySpringMass = 1.0

DonorRecoilSettings.SwayMultiplier = {
	Idle = 1.0,
	Moving = 1.6,
	Sprinting = 2.2,
	Aiming = 0.25,
	Crouching = 0.7,
}

DonorRecoilSettings.RotSpringFrequency = 12.5
DonorRecoilSettings.RotSpringDamping = 0.92
DonorRecoilSettings.RotSpringMass = 1.0

DonorRecoilSettings.BreathPosSpringFrequency = 0.8
DonorRecoilSettings.BreathPosSpringDamping = 0.65
DonorRecoilSettings.BreathPosSpringMass = 1.0

DonorRecoilSettings.BreathRotSpringFrequency = 0.65
DonorRecoilSettings.BreathRotSpringDamping = 0.55
DonorRecoilSettings.BreathRotSpringMass = 1.0

DonorRecoilSettings.BreathIntensity = {
	Position = 0.008,
	Rotation = 0.003,
}

DonorRecoilSettings.WalkPosSpringFrequency = 4.5
DonorRecoilSettings.WalkPosSpringDamping = 0.78
DonorRecoilSettings.WalkPosSpringMass = 1.0

DonorRecoilSettings.WalkRotSpringFrequency = 5.2
DonorRecoilSettings.WalkRotSpringDamping = 0.82
DonorRecoilSettings.WalkRotSpringMass = 1.0

DonorRecoilSettings.WalkBobIntensity = {
	Position = 0.025,
	Rotation = 0.012,

local DonorSpring = {}
DonorSpring.__index = DonorSpring

local abs = math.abs
local clamp = math.clamp

local DEFAULT_SNAP_DISTANCE = 0.001

local function isVector3(value)
	return typeof(value) == "Vector3"
end

local function zeroLike(value)
	if isVector3(value) then
		return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end
	return 0
end

local function magnitudeOf(value)
	if isVector3(value) then
		return value.Magnitude
	end
	return abs(value)
end

local function add(a, b)
	return a + b
end

local function subtract(a, b)
	return a - b
end

local function multiply(value, scalar)
	return value * scalar
end

function DonorSpring.new(frequency, damping, mass, initialValue, targetValue)
	local self = setmetatable({}, DonorSpring)

	local sample = initialValue
	if sample == nil then
		sample = targetValue
	end
	if sample == nil then
		sample = 0
	end

	local zero = zeroLike(sample)

	self.Frequency = frequency or 6
	self.Damping = damping or 0.75
	self.Mass = mass or 1.1
	self.MaxVelocity = nil
	self.SnapDistance = DEFAULT_SNAP_DISTANCE

	self.Position = initialValue ~= nil and initialValue or zero
	self.Velocity = zero
	self.Target = targetValue ~= nil and targetValue or zero

	return self
end

function DonorSpring:SetTarget(target)
	self.Target = target
end

function DonorSpring:SetFrequency(frequency)
	self.Frequency = frequency
end

function DonorSpring:SetDamping(damping)
	self.Damping = damping
end

function DonorSpring:SetMass(mass)
	self.Mass = mass
end

function DonorSpring:SetMaxVelocity(maxVelocity)
	self.MaxVelocity = maxVelocity
end

function DonorSpring:SetSnapDistance(snapDistance)
	self.SnapDistance = snapDistance
end

function DonorSpring:Shove(force)
	self.Velocity = add(self.Velocity, force)
end

function DonorSpring:Reset(value)
	local baseValue = value
	if baseValue == nil then
		baseValue = zeroLike(self.Position)
	end

	self.Position = baseValue
	self.Velocity = zeroLike(baseValue)
	self.Target = baseValue
end

function DonorSpring:GetPosition()
	return self.Position
end

function DonorSpring:GetVelocity()
	return self.Velocity
end

function DonorSpring:IsAtRest()
	local displacement = subtract(self.Position, self.Target)
	return magnitudeOf(displacement) < self.SnapDistance
		and magnitudeOf(self.Velocity) < self.SnapDistance
end

function DonorSpring:Update(dt)
	dt = clamp(dt or 0, 0.0001, 0.1)

	local frequency = self.Frequency
	local damping = self.Damping
	local mass = self.Mass

	local displacement = subtract(self.Position, self.Target)

	local accel = multiply(displacement, -(frequency * frequency))
	accel = add(accel, multiply(self.Velocity, -(2 * frequency * damping)))
	accel = multiply(accel, 1 / mass)

	self.Velocity = add(self.Velocity, multiply(accel, dt))

	if self.MaxVelocity ~= nil then
		if isVector3(self.Velocity) then
			local speed = self.Velocity.Magnitude
			if speed > self.MaxVelocity then
				self.Velocity = self.Velocity.Unit * self.MaxVelocity
			end
		else
			self.Velocity = clamp(self.Velocity, -self.MaxVelocity, self.MaxVelocity)
		end
	end

	self.Position = add(self.Position, multiply(self.Velocity, dt))

	local newDisplacement = subtract(self.Position, self.Target)
	if magnitudeOf(newDisplacement) < self.SnapDistance and magnitudeOf(self.Velocity) < self.SnapDistance then
		self.Position = self.Target
		self.Velocity = zeroLike(self.Position)
	end

	return self.Position
end

return DonorSpringlocal SE_Workspace 		= workspace:WaitForChild("ACS_WorkSpace")
local Debris 			= game:GetService("Debris")
local TS 				= game:GetService('TweenService')

-- HOLES SYSTEM

local Engine		= game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("ACS_Engine")
local HolesSource	= Engine:WaitForChild("Holes")

-- Збирає підпапки як { ["Hit"] = {ParticleEmitter1, ...}, ["Rico"] = {...} }
-- Тільки реальні ParticleEmitter — ніякого сміття (NumberValue, BoolValue тощо)
local function TypesCacheParticles(source)
	if not source then return nil end
	local profile = {}
	for _, folder in ipairs(source:GetChildren()) do
		local emitters = {}
		for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
			if child:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
				table.insert(emitters, child)
			end
		end
		if #emitters > 0 then
			profile[folder.Name] = emitters
		end
	end
	return next(profile) and profile or nil
end

-- Збирає підпапки як { ["Hit"] = {obj1, obj2}, ["Rico"] = {...} }
local function TypesCache(source)
	if not source then return nil end
	local profile = {}
	for _, folder in ipairs(source:GetChildren()) do
		profile[folder.Name] = folder:GetChildren()
	end
	return profile
end

local function MakeCache(source)
	local target = {}
	for _, folder in ipairs(source:GetChildren()) do
		target[folder.Name] = {
			Holes		= TypesCache(folder:FindFirstChild("Holes")),
			Particles	= TypesCacheParticles(folder:FindFirstChild("Particles")),
			FlashDecals	= TypesCache(folder:FindFirstChild("FlashDecals")),
			Sounds		= folder:FindFirstChild("Sounds") and folder.Sounds:GetChildren() or nil,
			HoleSize	= folder:FindFirstChild("HoleSize"),
			DontRecolor	= folder:FindFirstChild("DontRecolor") and folder.DontRecolor.Value or false,
		}
	end
	return target
end

local HolesCache = MakeCache(HolesSource)

-- Список частин тіла персонажа
local BodyParts = {
	Head=true, UpperTorso=true, LowerTorso=true,
	Torso=true, HumanoidRootPart=true,
	LeftHand=true, LeftLowerArm=true, LeftUpperArm=true,
	RightHand=true, RightLowerArm=true, RightUpperArm=true,
	LeftFoot=true, LeftLowerLeg=true, LeftUpperLeg=true,
	RightFoot=true, RightLowerLeg=true, RightUpperLeg=true,
	["Right Arm"]=true, ["Left Arm"]=true,
	["Right Leg"]=true, ["Left Leg"]=true,
}

-- Карта матеріалів Roblox → назва папки в Holes
local MaterialMap = {

	[Enum.Material.DiamondPlate]	= "Metal",

	[Enum.Material.Limestone]		= "Concrete",
	[Enum.Material.Asphalt]			= "Concrete",
	[Enum.Material.Sandstone]		= "Concrete",
	[Enum.Material.CrackedLava]		= "Concrete",
	[Enum.Material.Grass]			= "Grass",
	[Enum.Material.LeafyGrass]		= "Grass",
	[Enum.Material.Sand]			= "Grass",
	[Enum.Material.Ground]			= "Grass",
	[Enum.Material.Snow]			= "Grass",
	[Enum.Material.Mud]				= "Grass",

	[Enum.Material.Ice]				= "Glass",

	[Enum.Material.Plastic]			= "Concrete",
	[Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic]	= "Concrete",
	[Enum.Material.ForceField]		= "Metal",
}

local function GetMatFolder(Material, HitPart)
	if BodyParts[HitPart.Name] then
		return HolesCache["Human"] or HolesCache["Default"]
	end
	if HitPart.Parent and HitPart.Parent:IsA("Accessory") then
		return HolesCache["Human"] or HolesCache["Default"]
	end
	local folderName = MaterialMap[Material]
	if folderName and HolesCache[folderName] then
		return HolesCache[folderName]
	end
	return HolesCache["Default"]
end

local function PickRandom(list)
	if not list or #list == 0 then return nil end
	return list[math.random(1, #list)]
end

-- Безпечно повертає список по типу (Hit/Rico/Out) або fallback на Hit
local function GetEffectList(typeFolder, effectType)
	if not typeFolder then return nil end
	local byType = typeFolder[effectType]
	if byType and #byType > 0 then return byType end
	local byHit = typeFolder["Hit"]
	if byHit and #byHit > 0 then return byHit end
	return nil
end

-- Спавн дірки
local function CreateHole(matFolder, Position, Normal, Ray_Ignore)
	local decals = GetEffectList(matFolder.Holes, "Hit")
	if not decals then return end

	local holeSize = matFolder.HoleSize and matFolder.HoleSize.Value or 0.2

	local Marca = Instance.new("Part")
	Marca.Anchored		= true
	Marca.CanCollide	= false
	Marca.Transparency	= 1
	Marca.Size			= Vector3.new(holeSize, holeSize, 0.01)
	Marca.CFrame		= CFrame.new(Position, Position + Normal)
	Marca.Parent		= SE_Workspace.Server
	table.insert(Ray_Ignore, Marca)

	local Dec = PickRandom(decals):Clone()
	Dec.Parent = Marca
	Debris:AddItem(Marca, 20)
end

-- Спавн партиклів — всі емітери з папки ефекту
local function CreateParticles(matFolder, Attachment)
	local emitters = GetEffectList(matFolder.Particles, "Hit")
	if not emitters then return end

	local maxLifetime = 0
	for _, particle in ipairs(emitters) do
		local p = particle:Clone()
		p.Enabled = false
		p.Parent = Attachment

		local dur = 0.05
		if p:FindFirstChild("Duration") then
			dur = p.Duration.Value
		end

		p:Emit(p.Rate * dur)

		if p.Lifetime.Max > maxLifetime then
			maxLifetime = p.Lifetime.Max
		end
		Debris:AddItem(p, p.Lifetime.Max)
	end
end

-- Спавн звуку
local function CreateSound(matFolder, Attachment)
	local soundAsset = PickRandom(matFolder.Sounds)
	if not soundAsset then return end

	local S = soundAsset:Clone()
	S.Parent = Attachment
	S.PlaybackSpeed = math.random(34, 50) / 40
	S:Play()
	Debris:AddItem(S, (S.TimeLength or 2) + 1)
end

-- Спавн flash decal
local function CreateFlashDecal(matFolder, Attachment)
	local flashDecals = GetEffectList(matFolder.FlashDecals, "Hit")
	if not flashDecals then return end

	local flashAsset = PickRandom(flashDecals)
	if not flashAsset then return end

	local bg = Instance.new("BillboardGui", Attachment)
	bg.Adornee = Attachment
	local flashsize = math.random(10, 20) / 10
	bg.Size = UDim2.new(flashsize, 0, flashsize, 0)

	local flash = flashAsset:Clone()
	flash.Visible = true
	flash.Rotation = math.random(0, 360)
	flash.Size = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.05, 0)
	flash.Parent = bg

	flash:TweenSize(UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "Out", "Quad", 0.1)
	game.Debris:AddItem(bg, 0.1)
end

local Glass		= {"4668969235";"1565825075";"1565824613";"4668969774";"4668969602";"4668970218";"4668970412";}

local function FallbackSound(Attachment, SoundList, SpeedMin, SpeedMax)
	local S = Instance.new("Sound")
	S.Parent		= Attachment
	S.Volume		= math.random(20,30)/10
	S.MaxDistance	= 100
	S.EmitterSize	= 5
	S.PlaybackSpeed	= math.random(SpeedMin, SpeedMax)/40
	S.SoundId		= "rbxassetid://" .. SoundList[math.random(1, #SoundList)]
	S:Play()
end

local function FallbackSmoke(Attachment, Color, SizeEnd, SpeedMin, SpeedMax, Lifetime, EmitCount, SpreadAngle, Direction)
	local P = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	P.Enabled		= false
	P.Color			= ColorSequence.new(Color)
	P.LightEmission	= 0
	P.LightInfluence= 1
	P.Size			= NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.1,0.1); NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,SizeEnd)})
	P.Texture		= "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
	P.Transparency	= NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.75,0); NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1)})
	P.Lifetime		= NumberRange.new(Lifetime - 0.1, Lifetime + 0.1)
	P.Rate			= 1000
	P.Drag			= 20
	P.RotSpeed		= NumberRange.new(-360,360)
	P.Speed			= NumberRange.new(SpeedMin, SpeedMax)
	P.SpreadAngle	= SpreadAngle or Vector2.new(180,360)
	P.EmissionDirection = Direction or "Front"
	P.Parent		= Attachment
	P:Emit(EmitCount)
	Debris:AddItem(Attachment, P.Lifetime.Max)
end

local function FallbackFlash(Attachment, ImageId, SizeMin, SizeMax, FadeTime)
	local bg = Instance.new("BillboardGui", Attachment)
	bg.Adornee = Attachment
	bg.Size = UDim2.new(math.random(SizeMin, SizeMax)/10, 0, math.random(SizeMin, SizeMax)/10, 0)
	local flash = Instance.new("ImageLabel", bg)
	flash.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	flash.Size = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.05, 0)
	flash.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	flash.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=" .. ImageId
	flash.ImageTransparency = math.random(0, .5)
	flash.Rotation = math.random(0, 360)
	flash:TweenSizeAndPosition(UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), UDim2.new(0,0,0,0), "Out", "Quad", FadeTime)
	game.Debris:AddItem(bg, FadeTime)
end

local function FallbackBlood(Attachment, EmitCount)
	local P = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	P.Enabled		= false
	P.Color			= ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(125, 0, 0))
	P.LightEmission	= 0
	P.LightInfluence= 1
	P.Size			= NumberSequence.new(.25, 2.5)
	P.Texture		= "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
	P.Transparency	= NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.5,0); NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1)})
	P.Acceleration	= Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
	P.Lifetime		= NumberRange.new(.25, 1)
	P.Rate			= 2000
	P.Drag			= 10
	P.RotSpeed		= NumberRange.new(-150,150)
	P.Speed			= NumberRange.new(7, 15)
	P.VelocitySpread= math.random(2,20)
	P.SpreadAngle	= Vector2.new(-150,150)
	P.LockedToPart	= true
	P.EmissionDirection = "Front"
	P.Parent		= Attachment
	P:Emit(EmitCount)
	Debris:AddItem(Attachment, P.Lifetime.Max)
end

-- =============================================
-- ГОЛОВНА ФУНКЦІЯ
-- =============================================
local Hitmarker = {}

function Hitmarker.HitEffect(Ray_Ignore, Position, HitPart, Normal, Material, Settings)
	if HitPart == nil then return end

	local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", workspace.Terrain)
	Attachment.CFrame = CFrame.new(Position, Position + Normal)

	local matFolder = GetMatFolder(Material, HitPart)

	if matFolder and (matFolder.Particles or matFolder.Sounds or matFolder.FlashDecals) then
		-- ✅ НОВИЙ МЕТОД — читаємо з Holes
		CreateSound(matFolder, Attachment)
		CreateParticles(matFolder, Attachment)
		CreateFlashDecal(matFolder, Attachment)

		if not BodyParts[HitPart.Name] and not (HitPart.Parent and HitPart.Parent:IsA("Accessory")) then
			CreateHole(matFolder, Position, Normal, Ray_Ignore)
		end

		Debris:AddItem(Attachment, 3)

	else
		-- ⚠️ ЗАПАСНИЙ МЕТОД — стара логіка

		if HitPart.Name == "Head" or (HitPart.Parent and HitPart.Parent.Name == "Top") then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Hits, 34, 50)
			FallbackBlood(Attachment, 50)

		elseif BodyParts[HitPart.Name] or (HitPart.Parent and (HitPart.Parent.Name == "Chest" or HitPart.Parent.Name == "Back")) then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Body, 34, 46)
			FallbackBlood(Attachment, 25)

		elseif HitPart.Parent and HitPart.Parent:IsA("Accessory") then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Hits, 34, 50)
			FallbackBlood(Attachment, 25)

		elseif HitPart.Name == "Glass" then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Glass, 32, 60)
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "5984841909", 10, 15, 0.1)
			HitPart:Destroy()

		elseif HitPart.Name == "DoorHinge" and Settings and Settings.CanBreachDoor then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Metal, 38, 58)
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "233113663", 15, 30, 0.15)
			HitPart:Destroy()
			local DoorModel = HitPart.Parent
			if DoorModel and DoorModel:FindFirstChild("DoorHinge") == nil then
				if DoorModel:FindFirstChild("Hinge") then DoorModel.Hinge:Destroy() end
			end

		elseif HitPart.Name == "Knob" and Settings and Settings.CanBreachDoor then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Metal, 38, 58)
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "233113663", 15, 30, 0.15)
			local DoorModel = HitPart
			if DoorModel.Parent and DoorModel.Parent:FindFirstChild("Hinge") then
				DoorModel.Parent.Hinge.HingeConstraint.ActuatorType = Enum.ActuatorType.Motor
			end
			HitPart:Destroy()

		elseif Material == Enum.Material.CorrodedMetal or Material == Enum.Material.Metal or Material == Enum.Material.DiamondPlate then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Metal, 38, 58)
			local P = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
			P.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255,150,0))
			P.LightEmission = 1; P.LightInfluence = 0
			P.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
			P.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,.25,0); NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,.1)})
			P.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0,-50,0)
			P.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.1, 0.65)
			P.Drag = 10
			P.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-360,360)
			P.Speed = NumberRange.new(25,75)
			P.VelocitySpread = math.random(5,20)
			P.EmissionDirection = "Front"
			P.Parent = Attachment
			P:Emit(50)
			Debris:AddItem(Attachment, P.Lifetime.Max)
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "233113663", 15, 30, 0.15)

		elseif Material == Enum.Material.Glass or Material == Enum.Material.Ice or Material == Enum.Material.Glacier then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Glass, 32, 60)
			FallbackSmoke(Attachment, Color3.new(50,50,50), 1, 25, 25, 0.5, 5, nil, "Front")
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "476778304", 10, 15, 0.1)

		elseif Material == Enum.Material.Wood or Material == Enum.Material.WoodPlanks then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Wood, 38, 50)
			FallbackSmoke(Attachment, HitPart.Color, 0.25, 30, 40, 0.5, 10, nil, "Front")
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "476778304", 10, 15, 0.1)

		elseif Material == Enum.Material.Grass or Material == Enum.Material.Sand or Material == Enum.Material.Ground or Material == Enum.Material.Snow or Material == Enum.Material.Mud or Material == Enum.Material.LeafyGrass or Material == Enum.Material.Fabric then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Grass, 38, 50)
			FallbackSmoke(Attachment, workspace.Terrain:GetMaterialColor(Material), 5, 15, 30, 0.75, 25, nil, "Front")
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "476778304", 10, 15, 0.1)

		elseif Material == Enum.Material.Concrete or Material == Enum.Material.Slate or Material == Enum.Material.Brick or Material == Enum.Material.Granite or Material == Enum.Material.Basalt or Material == Enum.Material.Rock or Material == Enum.Material.Limestone or Material == Enum.Material.Asphalt or Material == Enum.Material.Sandstone or Material == Enum.Material.CrackedLava then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Concrete, 38, 46)
			FallbackSmoke(Attachment, Color3.new(50,50,50), 2, 15, 30, 0.75, 50, nil, "Front")
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "476778304", 10, 15, 0.1)

		elseif Material == Enum.Material.ForceField then
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Whizz, 32, 50)
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "233113663", 15, 30, 0.15)
			Debris:AddItem(Attachment, 1)

		else
			FallbackSound(Attachment, Cracks, 32, 50)
			FallbackSmoke(Attachment, Color3.new(50,50,50), 2, 15, 30, 0.75, 50, nil, "Front")
			FallbackFlash(Attachment, "476778304", 10, 15, 0.1)
		end
	end

	-- Логіка мішені (без змін)
	if HitPart.Name == "Hitmaker" then
		local Marca = Instance.new("Part")
		Marca.Material		= Enum.Material.Neon
		Marca.Anchored		= true
		Marca.CanCollide	= false
		Marca.Color			= Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)
		Marca.Size			= Vector3.new(0.2,0.2,0.01)
		Marca.Parent		= SE_Workspace.Server
		Marca.CFrame		= CFrame.new(Position, Position + Normal)
		TS:Create(Marca, TweenInfo.new(5,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut,0,false,5), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,255)}):Play()
		TS:Create(Marca, TweenInfo.new(5,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut,0,false,15), {Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(0,0,0.01)}):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(Attachment, 5)
		game.Debris:AddItem(Marca, 20)

	elseif HitPart.Name == "alvo" then
		local Marca = Instance.new("Part")
		Marca.Anchored		= true
		Marca.CanCollide	= false
		Marca.Transparency	= 1
		Marca.Size			= Vector3.new(0.2,0.2,0.01)
		Marca.Parent		= SE_Workspace.Server
		Marca.CFrame		= CFrame.new(Position, Position + Normal)
		Debris:AddItem(Attachment, 5)
		game.Debris:AddItem(Marca, 20)
		table.insert(Ray_Ignore, Marca)
		local Dec = Instance.new("Decal")
		Dec.Texture = "rbxassetid://359667865"
		Dec.Parent = Marca
	end
end

-- ВИБУХ (без змін)

function Hitmarker.Explosion(Position, HitPart, Normal)
	local Explosion_SFX = {"287390459";"287390954";"287391087";"287391197";"287391361";"287391499";"287391567";}

	local Hitmark = Instance.new("Attachment")
	Hitmark.CFrame = CFrame.new(Position, Position + Normal)
	Hitmark.Parent = workspace.Terrain

	local S = Instance.new("Sound")
	S.EmitterSize		= 50
	S.MaxDistance		= 1500
	S.SoundId			= "rbxassetid://" .. Explosion_SFX[math.random(1,7)]
	S.PlaybackSpeed		= math.random(30,55)/40
	S.Volume			= 2
	S.Parent			= Hitmark
	S.PlayOnRemove		= true
	S:Destroy()

	local Exp = Instance.new("Explosion")
	Exp.BlastPressure				= 0
	Exp.BlastRadius					= 0
	Exp.DestroyJointRadiusPercent	= 0
	Exp.Position					= Hitmark.Position
	Exp.Parent						= Hitmark

	Debris:AddItem(Hitmark, 5)
end

return Hitmarker

local RecoilCamera = {}
RecoilCamera.__index = RecoilCamera

local abs = math.abs
local min = math.min
local clamp = math.clamp

local function createSpring(stiffness, damping)
	return {
		position = 0,
		velocity = 0,
		stiffness = stiffness,
		damping = damping,
	}
end

local function updateSpring(spring, dt)
	local accel = -spring.stiffness * spring.position - spring.damping * spring.velocity
	spring.velocity = spring.velocity + accel * dt
	spring.position = spring.position + spring.velocity * dt

	if abs(spring.position) < 0.00008 and abs(spring.velocity) < 0.0009 then
		spring.position = 0
		spring.velocity = 0
	end

	return spring.position
end

local function addImpulse(spring, impulse, maxVelocity)
	spring.velocity = spring.velocity + impulse
	spring.velocity = clamp(spring.velocity, -maxVelocity, maxVelocity)
end

function RecoilCamera.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, RecoilCamera)

	-- Увеличил stiffness для "резкости" и подобрал damping для быстрого возврата
	-- Теперь камера будет "отпрыгивать" быстрее
	self.pitchSpring = createSpring(120, 16) 
	self.yawSpring = createSpring(100, 14)
	self.rollSpring = createSpring(90, 12)

	-- Поднял множители силы, чтобы отдача была заметнее
	self.pitchKickScale = 1.35 
	self.yawKickScale = 1.0
	self.rollKickScale = 0.7

	-- Увеличил лимиты скорости, чтобы камера успевала за резким импульсом
	self.maxPitchVelocity = 0.35
	self.maxYawVelocity = 0.25
	self.maxRollVelocity = 0.2

	return self
end

function RecoilCamera:Recoil(x, y, z)
	x = x or 0
	y = y or 0
	z = z or 0

	local pitchImpulse = x * self.pitchKickScale
	local yawImpulse = y * self.yawKickScale
	local rollImpulse = z * self.rollKickScale

	rollImpulse = rollImpulse + yawImpulse * 0.25

	addImpulse(self.pitchSpring, pitchImpulse, self.maxPitchVelocity)
	addImpulse(self.yawSpring, yawImpulse, self.maxYawVelocity)
	addImpulse(self.rollSpring, rollImpulse, self.maxRollVelocity)
end

function RecoilCamera:Update(dt)
	dt = min(dt, 0.033)

	local pitch = updateSpring(self.pitchSpring, dt)
	local yaw = updateSpring(self.yawSpring, dt)
	local roll = updateSpring(self.rollSpring, dt)

	if abs(pitch) < 0.00002 then pitch = 0 end
	if abs(yaw) < 0.00002 then yaw = 0 end
	if abs(roll) < 0.00002 then roll = 0 end

	return CFrame.Angles(pitch, yaw, roll)
end

function RecoilCamera:Reset()
	self.pitchSpring.position = 0
	self.pitchSpring.velocity = 0

	self.yawSpring.position = 0
	self.yawSpring.velocity = 0

	self.rollSpring.position = 0
	self.rollSpring.velocity = 0
end

return RecoilCamera--!strict

local RecoilSystem = {}
RecoilSystem.__index = RecoilSystem

local min = math.min
local max = math.max
local abs = math.abs
local clamp = math.clamp
local sin = math.sin
local cos = math.cos
local random = math.random
local clock = os.clock

local function zeroIfSmall(position, velocity, posEps, velEps)
	if abs(position) < posEps and abs(velocity) < velEps then
		return 0, 0
	end
	return position, velocity
end

function RecoilSystem.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, RecoilSystem)

	self.pitch = 0
	self.yaw = 0
	self.roll = 0

	self.pitchVel = 0
	self.yawVel = 0
	self.rollVel = 0

	self.posX = 0
	self.posY = 0
	self.posZ = 0

	self.velX = 0
	self.velY = 0
	self.velZ = 0

	self.shotsFired = 0
	self.sprayAmount = 0
	self.lastShotTime = 0
	self.patternIndex = 0
	self.bounceDirection = 1

	self.currentProfile = nil

	return self
end

function RecoilSystem:SetProfile(profile)
	self.currentProfile = profile
end

function RecoilSystem:Kick(profile, multiplier)
	multiplier = multiplier or 1
	profile = profile or self.currentProfile or {}

	local currentTime = clock()
	local timeSinceLastShot = currentTime - self.lastShotTime

	local sprayDecayTime = profile.sprayDecayTime or 0.4
	if timeSinceLastShot > sprayDecayTime then
		self.sprayAmount = max(0, self.sprayAmount - timeSinceLastShot * 7.0)
		if self.sprayAmount < 0.05 then
			self.sprayAmount = 0
			self.shotsFired = 0
			self.patternIndex = 0
		end
	end

	self.lastShotTime = currentTime

	local firstShotMult = 1
	if self.shotsFired == 0 and profile.firstShotMultiplier then
		firstShotMult = profile.firstShotMultiplier
	end

	self.shotsFired += 1

	local sprayIncrease = profile.sprayIncrease or 0.052
	self.sprayAmount += sprayIncrease / (1 + self.shotsFired * 0.035)

	local kickStrength = profile.kickStrength or 0.08
	local kickRandomness = profile.kickRandomness or 0.3
	local verticalKick = kickStrength
		* (1 + (random() - 0.5) * kickRandomness)
		* firstShotMult
		* (1 + self.sprayAmount)

	local horizontalStrength = profile.horizontalStrength or 0.04
	local horizontalRandomness = profile.horizontalRandomness or 0.5
	local horizontalKick = horizontalStrength
		* (random() * 2 - 1)
		* (1 + (random() - 0.5) * horizontalRandomness)

	if profile.usePattern and profile.pattern and #profile.pattern > 0 then
		self.patternIndex = min(self.patternIndex + 1, #profile.pattern)
		local patternPoint = profile.pattern[self.patternIndex]
		local patternScale = profile.patternScale or 1
		local patternRandomness = profile.patternRandomness or 0.1
		local patternNoise = 1 + (random() - 0.5) * patternRandomness * 0.8

		verticalKick = patternPoint.y * patternScale * patternNoise
		horizontalKick = patternPoint.x * patternScale * patternNoise
	end

	if profile.horizontalBounce then
		local bounceAmount = profile.bounceAmount or 0.7
		horizontalKick *= self.bounceDirection * bounceAmount
		self.bounceDirection = -self.bounceDirection
	end

	local patternSpeed = profile.patternSpeed or 20
	local speedModifier = patternSpeed * (min(timeSinceLastShot, 0.1) * 2 + 0.8)

	verticalKick *= multiplier * speedModifier
	horizontalKick *= multiplier * speedModifier

	local instant = profile.instantKickRatio or 0
	self.pitch += verticalKick * instant
	self.yaw += horizontalKick * instant

	verticalKick *= (1 - instant)
	horizontalKick *= (1 - instant)

	local verticalMult = profile.verticalMult or 1
	local horizontalMult = profile.horizontalMult or 1
	local leftBias = profile.leftBias or 0

	verticalKick *= verticalMult
	horizontalKick = horizontalKick * horizontalMult + (leftBias * speedModifier * multiplier)

	self.pitchVel += verticalKick
	self.yawVel += horizontalKick

	local rollStrength = profile.rollStrength or 0.02
	self.rollVel += (rollStrength * (random() * 2 - 1)) * (multiplier * speedModifier) * 0.7 - horizontalKick * 0.25

	local gunKickback = profile.gunKickback or -0.038
	local gunRise = profile.gunRise or 0.04
	local posMult = profile.posMult or 1
	local posInstant = profile.posInstantKickRatio or instant

	self.posZ += gunKickback * speedModifier * 0.45 * multiplier * posMult * posInstant
	self.posY += gunRise * speedModifier * 0.30 * multiplier * posMult * posInstant

	self.velZ += gunKickback * speedModifier * 1.1 * multiplier * posMult
	self.velY += gunRise * speedModifier * 0.62 * multiplier * posMult
	self.velX += horizontalKick * 0.18 * posMult
end

function RecoilSystem:Update(dt)
	dt = min(dt, 0.033)

	local profile = self.currentProfile or {}

	local returnSpeed = profile.returnSpeed or 0.17
	local returnSmoothness = profile.returnSmoothness or 0.8

	local springStiffness = profile.springStiffness or 75.0
	local springDamping = profile.springDamping or 18.0

	springStiffness = springStiffness * (0.92 + returnSpeed * 0.55)
	springDamping = springDamping * (0.92 + returnSmoothness * 0.22)

	local function updateSpring(position, velocity, posEps, velEps)
		local acceleration = (-springStiffness * position - springDamping * velocity) * dt
		local newVelocity = velocity + acceleration
		local newPosition = position + newVelocity * dt
		return zeroIfSmall(newPosition, newVelocity, posEps, velEps)
	end

	self.pitch, self.pitchVel = updateSpring(self.pitch, self.pitchVel, 0.00055, 0.0055)
	self.yaw, self.yawVel = updateSpring(self.yaw, self.yawVel, 0.00055, 0.0055)
	self.roll, self.rollVel = updateSpring(self.roll, self.rollVel, 0.00055, 0.0055)

	if self.pitch < 0 and self.pitchVel < 0 then
		self.pitchVel *= 0.28
	end

	self.posX, self.velX = updateSpring(self.posX, self.velX, 0.00045, 0.0045)
	self.posY, self.velY = updateSpring(self.posY, self.velY, 0.00045, 0.0045)
	self.posZ, self.velZ = updateSpring(self.posZ, self.velZ, 0.00045, 0.0045)

	self.sprayAmount = max(0, self.sprayAmount - dt * 4.8)

	local minPitch = profile.minPitch or -0.12
	local maxPitch = profile.maxPitch or 0.85
	local maxYaw = profile.maxYaw or 0.35
	local maxRoll = profile.maxRoll or 0.25

	self.pitch = clamp(self.pitch, minPitch, maxPitch)
	self.yaw = clamp(self.yaw, -maxYaw, maxYaw)
	self.roll = clamp(self.roll, -maxRoll, maxRoll)

	local camShake = profile.cameraShake or 0.04
	local currentTime = clock()
	local shakeIntensity = (abs(self.pitchVel) + abs(self.yawVel)) * camShake * 0.006

	local positionalCF = CFrame.new(
		self.posX * 0.22 + sin(currentTime * 36.0) * shakeIntensity,
		self.posY * 0.30 + cos(currentTime * 29.0) * shakeIntensity,
		self.posZ * 0.36
	)

	local rotationalCF = CFrame.Angles(self.pitch, self.yaw, self.roll)

	return positionalCF * rotationalCF
end

function RecoilSystem:GetCameraRecoil()
	return Vector2.new(self.yaw * 45, -self.pitch * 45)
end

function RecoilSystem:Reset()
	self.pitch = 0
	self.yaw = 0
	self.roll = 0

	self.pitchVel = 0
	self.yawVel = 0
	self.rollVel = 0

	self.posX = 0
	self.posY = 0
	self.posZ = 0

	self.velX = 0
	self.velY = 0
	self.velZ = 0

	self.shotsFired = 0
	self.sprayAmount = 0
	self.patternIndex = 0
	self.lastShotTime = 0
	self.bounceDirection = 1
end

function RecoilSystem:GetSprayAmount()
	return self.sprayAmount
end

function RecoilSystem:GetShotsFired()
	return self.shotsFired
end

return RecoilSystemlocal ShotBobble = {}
ShotBobble.__index = ShotBobble

local random = math.random
local sin = math.sin
local cos = math.cos
local pi = math.pi
local min = math.min
local exp = math.exp

local ZERO = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
local TAU = pi * 2

function ShotBobble.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, ShotBobble)

	self.intensity = 0
	self.phase = random() * TAU

	self.decayRate = 13.5
	self.frequency = 11.5
	self.maxIntensity = 0.42

	self.posLerpSpeed = 18
	self.rotLerpSpeed = 16

	self.currentPos = ZERO
	self.currentRot = ZERO

	return self
end

function ShotBobble:Trigger(amount)
	amount = amount or 1

	self.intensity = min(self.intensity + amount * 0.26, self.maxIntensity)
	self.phase = self.phase + (random() - 0.5) * 0.22
end

function ShotBobble:Update(dt, isMoving, isAiming)
	dt = math.min(dt, 0.033)

	self.intensity = self.intensity * exp(-self.decayRate * dt)
	if self.intensity < 0.0015 then
		self.intensity = 0
	end

	self.phase = self.phase + (self.frequency + self.intensity * 2.4) * dt

	local amount = self.intensity

	if isAiming then
		amount = amount * 0.12
	end

	if isMoving then
		amount = amount * 0.92
	end

	local targetPos = ZERO
	local targetRot = ZERO

	if amount > 0 then
		local waveA = sin(self.phase)
		local waveB = sin(self.phase * 1.35 + 0.35)
		local waveC = cos(self.phase * 0.82 + 0.6)

		targetPos = Vector3.new(
			(waveB * 0.00055) * amount,
			(waveA * 0.00165 + waveC * 0.00035) * amount,
			(cos(self.phase * 1.12 + 0.9) * 0.00045) * amount
		)

		targetRot = Vector3.new(
			(sin(self.phase * 0.92 + 0.1) * 0.00075) * amount,
			(sin(self.phase * 1.08 + 0.25) * 0.00042) * amount,
			(sin(self.phase * 0.74 + 0.5) * 0.00032) * amount
		)
	end

	local posAlpha = math.min(dt * self.posLerpSpeed, 1)
	local rotAlpha = math.min(dt * self.rotLerpSpeed, 1)

	self.currentPos = self.currentPos:Lerp(targetPos, posAlpha)
	self.currentRot = self.currentRot:Lerp(targetRot, rotAlpha)

	if self.currentPos.Magnitude < 0.00003 and self.currentRot.Magnitude < 0.00003 and self.intensity == 0 then
		self.currentPos = ZERO
		self.currentRot = ZERO
	end

	return CFrame.new(self.currentPos.X, self.currentPos.Y, self.currentPos.Z)
		* CFrame.Angles(self.currentRot.X, self.currentRot.Y, self.currentRot.Z)
end

function ShotBobble:Reset()
	self.intensity = 0
	self.currentPos = ZERO
	self.currentRot = ZERO
end

return ShotBobblelocal ShotViewKick = {}
ShotViewKick.__index = ShotViewKick

local random = math.random
local rad = math.rad
local min = math.min
local abs = math.abs

local ZERO = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

local function createSpring(stiffness, damping)
	return {
		position = ZERO,
		velocity = ZERO,
		stiffness = stiffness or 170,
		damping = damping or 24,
	}
end

local function updateSpringVec3(spring, dt)
	local accel = -spring.stiffness * spring.position - spring.damping * spring.velocity
	spring.velocity = spring.velocity + accel * dt
	spring.position = spring.position + spring.velocity * dt

	if spring.position.Magnitude < 0.00008 and spring.velocity.Magnitude < 0.0009 then
		spring.position = ZERO
		spring.velocity = ZERO
	end

	return spring.position
end

function ShotViewKick.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, ShotViewKick)

	self.posSpring = createSpring(165, 24)
	self.rotSpring = createSpring(145, 22)

	self.sideBias = -0.16

	return self
end

function ShotViewKick:FireKick(x, y, z)
	x = x or 0
	y = y or 0
	z = z or 0

	local lateral = x + self.sideBias * (0.65 + random() * 0.35)
	local vertical = y
	local depth = z

	self.posSpring.velocity = self.posSpring.velocity + Vector3.new(
		lateral * 1.15,
		vertical * 1.45,
		depth * 1.65
	)

	self.rotSpring.velocity = self.rotSpring.velocity + Vector3.new(
		(-vertical * 0.78) + rad(random(-3, 2) * 0.01),
		(lateral * 0.34),
		(lateral * 0.18) + (depth * 0.08)
	)
end

function ShotViewKick:Update(dt)
	dt = min(dt, 0.033)

	local pos = updateSpringVec3(self.posSpring, dt)
	local rot = updateSpringVec3(self.rotSpring, dt)

	local posX = pos.X * 0.20
	local posY = pos.Y * 0.30
	local posZ = pos.Z * 0.34

	local rotX = rot.X
	local rotY = rot.Y
	local rotZ = rot.Z

	if abs(posX) < 0.00002 then posX = 0 end
	if abs(posY) < 0.00002 then posY = 0 end
	if abs(posZ) < 0.00002 then posZ = 0 end

	if abs(rotX) < 0.00002 then rotX = 0 end
	if abs(rotY) < 0.00002 then rotY = 0 end
	if abs(rotZ) < 0.00002 then rotZ = 0 end

	local posCF = CFrame.new(posX, posY, posZ)
	local rotCF = CFrame.Angles(rotX, rotY, rotZ)

	return posCF * rotCF
end

function ShotViewKick:Reset()
	self.posSpring.position = ZERO
	self.posSpring.velocity = ZERO
	self.rotSpring.position = ZERO
	self.rotSpring.velocity = ZERO
end

return ShotViewKicklocal Spring = {}

function Spring.new(Initial)
	local t0 = tick()
	local p0 = Initial or 0
	local v0 = Initial and 0*Initial or 0
	local t	= Initial or 0
	local d	= 1
	local s	= 1

	local function positionVelocity(Tick)
		local x	= Tick - t0
		local c0 = p0 - t
		if s == 0 then
			return p0, 0
		elseif d < 1 then
			local c	= math.sqrt(1 - d ^ 2)
			local c1 = (v0 / s + d * c0) / c
			local co = math.cos(c * s * x)
			local si = math.sin(c * s * x)
			local e	= math.exp(d * s * x)
			return t + (c0 * co + c1 * si) / e,
				s * ((c * c1 - d * c0) * co - (c * c0 + d * c1) * si) / e
		else
			local c1 = v0 / s + c0
			local e	= math.exp(s * x)
			return t + (c0 + c1 * s * x) / e,
				s * (c1 - c0 - c1 * s * x) / e
		end
	end

	return setmetatable(
		{
			accelerate = function(_, acceleration)
				local T = tick()
				local p, v = positionVelocity(T)
				p0 = p
				v0 = v + acceleration
				t0 = T
			end;
		},
		{
			__index = function(_, index)
				if index == "value" or index == "position" or index == "p" then
					local p, v = positionVelocity(tick())
					return p
				elseif index == "velocity" or index == "v" then
					local p, v = positionVelocity(tick())
					return v
				elseif index == "acceleration" or index == "a" then
					local x	= tick() - t0
					local c0 = p0 - t
					if s == 0 then
						return 0
					elseif d < 1 then
						local c = math.sqrt(1 - d ^ 2)
						local c1 = (v0 / s + d * c0) / c
						local cs = (c0 * d ^ 2 - 2 * c * d * c1 - c0 * c ^ 2) * math.cos(c * s * x)
						local sn = (c1 * d ^ 2 + 2 * c * d * c0 - c1 * c ^ 2) * math.sin(c * s * x)
						return s ^ 2 *(cs + sn) / math.exp(d * s * x)
					else
						local c1 = v0 / s + c0
						return s ^ 2 * (c0 - 2 * c1 + c1 * s * x) / math.exp(s * x)
					end
				elseif index == "target" or index == "t" then
					return t
				elseif index == "damper" or index == "d" then
					return d
				elseif index == "speed" or index == "s" then
					return s
				elseif index == "magnitude" or index == "m" then
					local p, v = positionVelocity(tick())
					return p.magnitude
				else
					error(index.." is not a valid member of spring", 0)
				end
			end;
			
			__newindex = function(_, index, value)
				local T = tick()
				if index == "value" or index == "position" or index == "p" then
					local p, v = positionVelocity(T)
					p0, v0 = value, v
				elseif index == "velocity" or index == "v" then
					local p, v = positionVelocity(T)
					p0, v0 = p, value
				elseif index == "acceleration" or index == "a" then
					local p, v = positionVelocity(T)
					p0, v0 = p, v + value
				elseif index == "target" or index == "t" then
					p0, v0 = positionVelocity(T)
					t = value
				elseif index == "damper" or index == "d" then
					p0, v0 = positionVelocity(T)
					d = value < 0 and 0 or value < 1 and value or 1
				elseif index == "speed" or index == "s" then
					p0, v0 = positionVelocity(T)
					s = value < 0 and 0 or value
				else
					error(index.." is not a valid member of spring", 0)
				end
				t0 = T
			end;
		}
	)
end

return Spring--!strict

local SurfaceResponse = {}

local WALLBANG_CONSTANT = 1

local WALLBANG_MATERIAL_HARDNESS = {
	default = 8,

	Concrete = 45,
	Brick = 55,
	Basalt = 45,
	Asphalt = 45,
	Pavement = 45,
	Cobblestone = 45,
	Rock = 45,
	Slate = 45,
	Granite = 45,
	Limestone = 45,
	Pebble = 45,

	Wood = 20.45,
	WoodPlanks = 20.28,
	Cardboard = 20.20,

	Metal = 35,
	DiamondPlate = 24,
	CorrodedMetal = 14,

	Grass = 0.30,
	LeafyGrass = 0.30,
	Dirt = 2.5,
	Ground = 2.5,
	Mud = 2.2,
	Sand = 1.8,
	Snow = 0.9,
	Fabric = 35.10,
	Carpet = 8,
	Leather = 10,
	Neon = 10,
	Water = 2.0,
	Rubber = 12,

	Plastic = 29.18,
	SmoothPlastic = 20.18,
	Glass = 20.05,
	Glacier = 20.12,
	Ice = 20.10,
}

local WALLBANG_SPECIAL_NAMES = {
	BulletproofGlass = 2,
	Armor = 10,
	EShield = 10,
	Hitmaker = 1000,
	Invincible = 1000,
}

local function getHardness(hitPart, material)
	if hitPart and WALLBANG_SPECIAL_NAMES[hitPart.Name] then
		return WALLBANG_SPECIAL_NAMES[hitPart.Name]
	end

	if material and WALLBANG_MATERIAL_HARDNESS[material.Name] ~= nil then
		return WALLBANG_MATERIAL_HARDNESS[material.Name]
	end

	return WALLBANG_MATERIAL_HARDNESS.default
end

function SurfaceResponse.Resolve(hitResult, velocity, bulletPower, resolvedBallistics)
	local hitPart = hitResult.Instance
	if not hitPart or not hitPart:IsA("BasePart") then
		return { Type = "Stop" }
	end

	local penetrationIndex = resolvedBallistics.PenetrationIndex or 0
	if penetrationIndex <= 0 or bulletPower <= 0 then
		return { Type = "Stop" }
	end

	local hardness = getHardness(hitPart, hitResult.Material)
	if hardness >= 1000 then
		return { Type = "Stop" }
	end

	local castDist = if hardness <= 0 then 6 else (penetrationIndex / hardness)
	local castDist2 = castDist * bulletPower * WALLBANG_CONSTANT

	if castDist2 <= 0.01 then
		return { Type = "Stop" }
	end

	local rayDir = velocity.Unit

	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { hitPart }
	params.IgnoreWater = true

	local startPos = hitResult.Position + rayDir * castDist2
	local backVec = -rayDir * castDist2

	local exitRay = workspace:Raycast(startPos, backVec, params)
	if not exitRay then
		return { Type = "Stop" }
	end

	local thickness = (exitRay.Position - hitResult.Position).Magnitude
	local newBulletPower = math.max(bulletPower - (thickness / castDist2), 0)

	if newBulletPower <= 0.05 then
		return { Type = "Stop" }
	end

	return {
		Type = "Penetrate",
		ExitPosition = exitRay.Position + rayDir * 0.05,
		Thickness = thickness,
		BulletPower = newBulletPower,
	}
end

return SurfaceResponselocal Thread = {}

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

function Thread:Wait(t)
	if t ~= nil then
		local TotalTime = 0
		TotalTime = TotalTime + RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		while TotalTime < t do
			TotalTime = TotalTime + RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
	else
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
end

function Thread:Spawn(callback)
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	bindable.Event:connect(callback)
	bindable:Fire()
	bindable:Destroy()
end

function Thread:Delay(t, callback)
	self:Spawn(function()
		self:Wait(t)
		callback()
	end)
end

return Threadlocal Utilities = {}

Utilities.Weld = function(p1, p2, c0, c1)
	local w = Instance.new("Motor6D", p1)
	w.Part0 = p1
	w.Part1 = p2
	w.Name = p2.Name
	w.C0 = c0 or p1.CFrame:inverse() * p2.CFrame
	w.C1 = c1 or CFrame.new()
	return w
end

Utilities.WeldComplex = function(x,y,Name)
	local W = Instance.new("Motor6D")
	W.Name = Name
	W.Part0 = x
	W.Part1 = y
	local CJ = CFrame.new(x.Position)
	local C0 = x.CFrame:inverse()*CJ
	local C1 = y.CFrame:inverse()*CJ
	W.C0 = C0
	W.C1 = C1
	W.Parent = x
	return W
end

Utilities.CheckForHumanoid = function(search)
	local result = false
	local humanoid = nil
	if search then
		if (search.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or search.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")) then
			result = true
			if search.Parent:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') then
				humanoid = search.Parent.Humanoid
			elseif search.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') then
				humanoid = search.Parent.Parent.Humanoid
			end
		else
			result = false
		end	
	end
	return result,humanoid
end

return Utilitieslocal Spring = require(script.Parent.Spring)

local VMKick = {}
VMKick.__index = VMKick

function VMKick.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, VMKick)

	-- Пружина позиции (назад/вверх)
	self.Pos = Spring.new(Vector3.new())
	self.Pos.s = 70
	self.Pos.d = 0.75

	-- Пружина вращения (подброс ствола)
	self.Rot = Spring.new(Vector3.new())
	self.Rot.s = 85
	self.Rot.d = 0.75

	-- Точка вращения (важно для эффекта "ствол подбрасывает")
	self.Pivot = 0.18 -- 0.12..0.25 подбирай

	return self
end

function VMKick:FireKick(posImpulse: Vector3, rotImpulse: Vector3)
	-- Импульс в пружины
	self.Pos:accelerate(posImpulse)
	self.Rot:accelerate(rotImpulse)
end

function VMKick:Update(_dt)
	-- _dt не нужен, Spring сам считает от tick()
	local p = self.Pos.p
	local r = self.Rot.p

	local pivot = self.Pivot

	return CFrame.new(p.X, p.Y, p.Z)
		* CFrame.new(0, 0, pivot)
		* CFrame.Angles(r.X, r.Y, r.Z)
		* CFrame.new(0, 0, -pivot)
end

return VMKicklocal TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
